6. Fair play 2: Quantity of red cards.
7. Lot.

Tie-breaks used in groups of three or four teams:
 1 Head-to-head points.
 2 Head-to-head goal difference.
 3 Head-to-head goals for.
 4 Head-to-head goals for as away.
 5 Repeat 1-4 if is necessary.
 6 Goal difference in the group.
 7 Goals for in the group.
 8 Goals for as away in the group.
 9 Repeat 6-8 if is necessary.
10 Lot.

Tie-breaks used in elimination rounds:
 1 Goal difference.
 2 Penalty Shot Out (PSO).
Tie-break used in Final Stage:
1 Penalty Shot Out (PSO).
